def soft_objective_grad(theta, L, n, m, temp):
    a = softmax_center(theta)
    c = conv_self(a,L)
    cmax = c.max()
    z = (c-cmax)/temp
    z = np.clip(z, -50, 0) # if temp small, differences negative
    u = np.exp(z); u/=sum(u)
    J = cmax + temp*np.log(u.sum())
    g = pressure(u,a)
    gtheta = a*(g - np.dot(a,g))
    gtheta -= gtheta.mean()? With softmax centering: if we compute a from centered theta, gradient wrt theta = P gθ. So do gtheta -= gtheta.mean().
    return J, gtheta

Detailed helper implementations:

Normalize:
```
def _normalize(a, eps=1e-300):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    a[np.isnan(a)|np.isinf(a)]=0
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       a.fill(1.0); s=a.sum()
    a/=s
    #maybe set very small to 0? Keep positive for log.
    return a
```

Conv:
```
def _conv_self(a,L):
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)
    c=c[:2*a.size-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0)
    return c
```
Potential negative central? max positive. Use `np.ascontiguousarray`.

Score: `2.0*n*np.max(c)`.

Max weights:
```
def _weights_from_v(v, temp):
    vmax=v.max()
    z=(v-vmax)/temp
    z=np.clip(z,-700,0)
    w=np.exp(z)
    ws=w.sum()
    if ws<=0 or not finite: w.fill(1.0); ws=w.size
    else: w/=ws
    return w, float(vmax)
```
Use `temp>0`. If very small, only max weights ~1. For multiple active, good.

Pressure:
```
def _pressure(u,a,L):
    U=np.fft.rfft(u,L)
    Ar=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],L)
    y=np.fft.irfft(U*Ar,L)
    g=2.0*y[n-1:n-1+n]
    np.maximum(g,0,out=g)
    return g
```
Check: If u weights sum1, g positive. For v weights, pressure derivative of objective in c? If objective c-based, g=pressure. Good.

Simplex projection:
```
def _project_simplex(v, tol=1e-15):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=v.size; u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)-1.0
    pos=np.where(u*(np.arange(1,n+1))-1.0>cssv)[0]
    if len(pos)==0: return np.maximum(v,0) maybe? Actually formula.
    idx=pos[-1]
    theta=cssv[idx]/(idx+1)
    out=np.clip(v-theta,0,None)
    s=out.sum(); if s>0: out/=s else out.fill(1/n)
    return out
```

Mirror update:
```
def _mirror_accept(a,g,cand):
   d=g-g.mean()
   scale=np.max(np.abs(d))
   if scale<1e-300: return None
   d=d/scale
   out=a*np.exp(np.clip(-cand*d,-70,70))
   return _normalize(out)
```
If d positive => reduce; negative increase.

Euclidean update:
```
def _euc_step(a,g,step):
   d=g-g.mean()
   # scale to probability step: max abs d -> 1
   scale=np.max(np.abs(d)); if scale>0: d/=scale
   v=a-step*d
   v=_project_simplex(v)
   return v
```

```

best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
```

L-BFGS implementation details:
Top-level `_lbfgs_fun(theta, L, n, m, temp_v)` returns f, grad. Need get a via softmax with centering. To avoid repeated mean? Stabilize:
```
def _softmax_center(theta):
    t=theta - np.mean(theta)
    tmax=t.max(); e=np.exp(t-tmax); s=e.sum(); return e/s
```
But inside fun, calling mean each time. If theta grows, subtract mean not change. Use stable.
`e` values can under/overflow; if t-tmax<-700 exp0. Sum at least 1.
Objective:
```
a=_softmax_center(theta)
c=_conv_self(a,L)
v=n*c
vmax=v.max()
z=(v-vmax)/temp_v; clip
u=np.exp(z); us=u.sum(); if us... 
f=(vmax + temp_v*math.log(us))/n  # c-based objective
# pressure with u, L
g=_pressure(u,a,L) # derivative wrt a for c-based
dg=np.dot(a,g)
grad=a*(g-dg)
grad -= grad.mean()  # because centered softmax? Let's verify.
```
If a=softmax(theta - mean theta), then d f/d theta_i = a_i * (g_i - sum a_j g_j) - mean_i? Actually theta_i appears in centered t_j=theta_j - mean(theta). For each i, ∂t_j/∂theta_i = δ_{ij} - 1/n. def _adam_search(a, L,n,m,rng,time_limit,temp_v0=0.2,temp_v1=0.01,lr0=0.2):
    eps=1e-15/n
    theta=np.log(np.maximum(a,eps)); theta-=theta.mean()
    mth=np.zeros(n); vth=np.zeros(n); t=0
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=...
    while time:
       # decay temp_v based on time or iters
       f? We only need exact score for accept, not soft value. But Adam requires gradient. Use gradient from weights with current temp.
       c=_conv_self(a); v=n*c; w,_=_weights_from_v(v,temp_v); g=_pressure(w,a,L); h=a*(g-dot(a,g)); h-=h.mean()
       t+=1; mth=0.9*mth+0.1*h; vth=0.999*vth+0.001*h*h; minh=mth/(1-0.9**t); vthh=vth/(1-0.999**t); theta += lr * minh/ (sqrt(vthh)+1e-8)
       a=_softmax_center(theta) # or normalize(exp(theta))? softmax center.
       score=2n*convmax (we can use v.max() from before? after update recompute c maybe)
       if score<best...
       if no improve > 50: lr*=0.7; temp_v*=0.9; if stuck perturb.
```

Let's derive `_random_prior` better for initial. We want nonuniform nonnegative. Use a mixture of low-frequency components and noise:
```
def _random_prior(n, rng):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    x=rng.normal(size=n, scale=0.3)
    # smooth via FFT? Simple moving average
    win=int(3+5*rng.random()); k=np.ones(win)/win; x=np.convolve(x,k,mode='same'); x/=max(std,1)
    z=0.0
    ncomp=4
    for _ in range(ncomp):
       f=rng.integers(1,8)
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2π); amp=rng.uniform(0.1,1.0)/sqrt(f)
       z += amp*np.cos(2π f t + ph) or sin
    z += 0.8*x
    a=np.exp(z-z.max()); normalize
```

```
def _active_search(a, L, n, m, rng, time_limit, temp0=0.2, temp_min=0.03, verbose=False):
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    a=_normalize(a)
    c=_conv_self(a,L); score=2*n*np.max(c)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    eta=0.7
    temp_v=temp0
    no_improve=0; reset_count=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        if it>0: # recompute c? We can keep current c if we accepted candidate, but easier compute each loop.
        # Actually after previous accept, we know current c? Let's compute c and score.
        c=_conv_self(a,L); score=...
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
        # if score worsens due to perturbation? okay.
        v=n*c
        # weights with temp_v
        w,vmax=_weights_from_v(v,temp_v)
        neff=1/(w@w)
        if neff<6: temp_v=min(1.0, temp_v*1.3)
        elif neff>300: temp_v=max(temp_min, temp_v*0.85)
        g=_pressure(w,a,L)
        d=g-g.mean(); scale=np.max(np.abs(d))
        if scale<=1e-300:
            temp_v*=0.9; it+=1; continue
        d/=scale
        # mirror candidates
        cand_a=None; cand_s=score
        for step in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25):
             a2=_mirror_update(a,d,step)
             s2=_score_self(a2,L,n,m)
             if s2 < cand_s:
                 cand_s=cand_s? cand_s=s2; cand_a=a2; chosen=step
        # maybe Euclidean for step eta*0.5
        for step in (eta*0.5, eta*0.25):
             a2=_euclidean_update(a,d,step)
             s2=...
             if s2<cand_s: ...
        if cand_a is not None and cand_s < score - 1e-13:
             a=cand_a; no_improve=0
             # adapt eta: if chosen one of larger steps increase
             if chosen > eta/2? etc.
             if it % 100 ==0: temp_v*=0.97
        else:
             eta=max(0.05, eta*0.75)
             no_improve+=1
             if no_improve>30:
                 # if score not best? reset to best and lower temp
                 if reset_count<5:
                     a=best_a.copy()
                     eta=0.7
                     temp_v=max(temp_min, temp_v*0.8)
                     reset_count+=1
                 else:
                     # perturb
                     rho=0.05+0.15*rng.random()
                     a=_normalize((1-rho)*a + rho*_random_prior(n,rng))
                     eta=0.7; temp_v=0.2; reset_count=0
                 no_improve=0
        it+=1
    # ensure best
    c=_conv_self(best_a,L); best_score=... 
    return best_a,best_score
```

def _score_self(a,L,n,m):

```
def _inverse_pressure_update(a,g,gamma):
   gmean = max(g.mean(), eps)
   ratio = gmean / np.maximum(g, eps)
   a2 = a * np.exp(gamma * np.log(np.clip(ratio, 1e-10, 1e10)))
   normalize
```
Equivalently a*i^(-gamma g/gmean). If g high, lower. This avoids arbitrary eta scale. It can be aggressive; line search gamma values (0.1,0.3,0.7,1). Could add to active search. But if g has zeros, ratio huge. Use eps = 1e-12; clip ratio.

Now, LP direction. Let's implement top-level `_lp_direction(a, c, n, K=120, U=None)`. It returns normalized g or None. Use scipy if available.
```
def _lp_direction(a, c, n, K=120):
    if _scipy_optimize is None: return None
    M=float(c.max())
    if M<=0: return None
    idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
    A=np.zeros((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r,k in enumerate(idx):
       start=max(0, k-(n-1)); end=min(n-1, k)
       if end<start: continue
       iidx=np.arange(start, end+1)
       A[r, iidx]=a[k-iidx]
    U=10.0*M + 1e-12
    cvec=np.ones(n)
    b=np.full(K, M)
    res=_scipy_optimize.linprog(cvec? max => c = -ones, A_ub=A,b_ub=b,bounds=(0,U),method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
    if res is not None and res.success and res.x is not None:
       g=np.maximum(res.x,0)
       g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0)
       s=g.sum()
       if s>0:
          return g/s
    # If fail, maybe use heuristics
    return None
```
But `linprog` minimizes c^T x, so c=-ones. Bounds specify sequence of tuples? `bounds=(0, U)` works for all? SciPy accepts tuple (lb,ub) applies all. Use list? Great.
A_ub dense; HIGHS may be slow. We can limit K=80. Maybe call only if time >60s. Add try/except; if exception return None.
Potential issue: The LP may return g with sum small or just at bounds for irrelevant positions. But line search mix. Good.

Random prior should be smooth to not waste. Use a few cosine terms with frequencies 1-6 and amplitudes decreasing.

```
def _random_prior(n, rng):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    z=np.zeros(n)
    ncomp=int(rng.integers(2,6))
    for r in range(ncomp):
       f=int(rng.integers(1,6))
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.2))/math.sqrt(f)
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       z += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*t + ph)
    noise=rng.normal(0,0.1,n)
    if n>5: noise=np.convolve(noise,np.ones(5)/5,mode='same')
    z += noise
    a=np.exp(z-z.max()); normalize
```
This may produce shapes.

```
def _weights_via_temperature(v, temp_v):
    vmax=v.max()
    if temp_v <= 0: # hard uniform top?
       return np.ones_like(v)/len(v), vmax
    z=(v-vmax)/temp_v
    z=np.clip(z,-700,0)
    w=np.exp(z)
    if w.sum()<=0: ...
    w/=w.sum()
    # If effective count too low, expand by uniform over indices with z > -5? or top K.
    neff=1/np.sum(w*w)
    if neff < 3:
       order=np.argsort(v)[::-1]
       K=max(int(5), int(0.005*v.size))
       top=np.zeros_like(w); top[order[:K]]=1.0; top/=K
       w=0.5*w+0.5*top
    elif neff > 500:
       # flatten to top 500? Maybe leave
    return w
```

def _lbfgs_polish(a, L,n,m, temp_v, time_limit):
    if _scipy_optimize is None: return None
    theta=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); theta-=theta.mean()
    stop_at=time.monotonic()+max(0,time_limit-2)
    _LBFGS_DEADLINE[0]=stop_at
    try:
       res=_scipy_optimize.minimize(_lbfgs_fun, theta, method='L-BFGS-B', args=(L,n,m,temp_v), jac=True, options={'maxiter':10000,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-10,'maxls':20,'disp':False}, callback=_lbfgs_callback)
    except Exception:
       return None
    a2=_softmax_center(res.x)
    # maybe take theta from res.x if finite
    return _normalize(a2)
```

def _perturb_and_search(a,L,n,m,rng,time_limit):
   best=a; bests=score(a)
   while time...:
      rho = 0.02 + 0.1*rng.random()
      # use low-frequency noise to stay smooth
      noise = _random_prior(n,rng)
      p = normalize((1-rho)*a + rho*noise)
      # or log-noise: theta=log a; add Fourier noise
      a2,b2 = _active_search(p, L,n,m,rng, min(60, remaining*0.2), temp0=0.15, temp_min=0.03)
      if b2<bests: update
   return best
```

- Loop: random i in high, j in low. Try δs. Evaluate score without recomputing weights. If accept, update a, c, score; if accepted >5 times, recompute weights/g.

Implement `_coordinate_polish`:
```
def _coordinate_polish(a,L,n,m,rng,time_limit,temp_v=0.1):
   deadline=...
   a=normalize; c=conv; score=...
   best...
   tries_no=0; recompute counter=0
   while time<deadline:
      if recompute counter...:
         v=n*c; w,_=weights(v,temp_v); g=pressure(w,a,L)
         # choose thresholds
         qt=np.quantile(g,[0.25,0.75])
         low=np.where(g<=qt[0])[0]; high=np.where(g>=qt[1])[0]
         if len(low)<10: low=np.argsort(g)[:100]; high=np.argsort(g)[-100:]
         recompute=0; temp_v*=0.95 if stuck?
         tries_no=0
      i=int(rng.integers(len(high))); j=int(rng.integers(len(low))); i=high[i]; j=low[j]
      if i==j: continue
      base=a[i]
      if base<=0: continue
      best_improve False
      for fac in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.75):
         delta=fac*base
         a2=a.copy(); a2[i]-=delta; a2[j]+=delta
         if a2[i]<0: continue
         s=score(a2)
         if s<score-1e-13:
             a=a2; score=s; update best; tries_no=0; break
      if not accepted: tries_no+=1; recompute+=1
      if tries_no>200: maybe perturb or break? recompute every 50 anyway.
```
But if high/low fixed, after many accepts g changes. Recompute when accepted >5 or no accept >50. Good.
This coordinate move may be more effective for exact max than mirror.

However trial a2 copies n array each; 10k copies of 6121 floats ~60MB total? okay. Could update in place and revert to avoid copy. But simpler. Time okay. We can optimize with in-place:

```
def _shape_candidates(n,rng):
    out=[]
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    x=2*t-1
    shapes=[]
    shapes.append(np.zeros(n))
    shapes.append(x)
    shapes.append(-x)
    shapes.append(x*x)
    shapes.append(-x*x)
    for f in (1,2,3,4,5,6):
       for amp in (0.6,1.0):
          for ph in (0, np.pi/2, np.pi, 3*np.pi/2):
             shapes.append(amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t+ph))
             # maybe cos
    # quadratic centered
    for c in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
       amp=1.5
       shapes.append(amp*(t-c)**2)
       shapes.append(-amp*(t-c)**2)
    for z in shapes:
       sc=np.max(np.abs(z)); if sc>0: z=z/sc*0.9
       p=np.exp(z-z.max()); p/=p.sum(); out.append(p)
    for _ in range(10): out.append(_random_prior(n,rng))
    return out
```
Could be many ~40. Evaluate each (FFT) okay. For n=6121, 40*conv ~0.2s. Good. Use max 2-3 temp? Fine.

def _coordinate_polish(a, L, n, m, rng, time_limit, temp_v=0.08):
    if time_limit <= 0: return a, _score_self(a,L,n,m)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    a=_normalize(a)
    c=_conv_self(a,L); score=...
    best...
    no_accept=0; recompute=0; accept_count=0; temp=temp_v
    high_idx=np.arange(n); low_idx=high_idx
    while time<deadline:
       if recompute >= 25 or no_accept >= 200 or accept_count >= 10:
          c=_conv_self(a,L); score=...
          if score < best... update best, accept_count=0
          v=n*c
          w,_=_weights_from_v(v,temp)
          g=_pressure(w,a,L)
          finite=True
          q25,q75=np.percentile(g,[25,75])
          low_idx=np.where(g <= q25 + 1e-300)[0]
          high_idx=np.where(g >= q75 - 1e-300)[0]
          if low_idx.size < 20: low_idx=np.argsort(g)[:max(20,n//100)]
          if high_idx.size < 20: high_idx=np.argsort(g)[::-1][:max(20,n//100)]
          recompute=0; accept_count=0
          if no_accept > 100:
              temp=max(0.02, temp*0.85)
          no_accept=0
       if high_idx.size==0 or low_idx.size==0: break
       i=int(high_idx[rng.integers(low_idx?)])
```

def _restart_search(a, L,n,m,rng,time_limit):
   deadline=...
   best=a; bests=score(a)
   attempts=0
   while time<deadline:
      attempts+=1
      rho = float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.25))
      # maybe use Fourier noise
      prior=_random_prior(n,rng)
      p=normalize((1-rho)*a+rho*prior)
      # short active
      lim=min(60, (deadline-time)/3)
      if lim<2: break
      a2,s2=_active_search(p,L,n,m,rng,lim,temp0=0.15,temp_min=0.03)
      if s2<bests: update
   return best,bests
```

`_inverse_pressure_update`:
```
def _inverse_pressure_update(a,g,gamma):
    g=np.maximum(g,1e-300)
    gm=float(np.mean(g))
    if gm <=0: return _normalize(a)
    ratio=gm/g
    logr=np.clip(np.log(ratio), -50, 50)
    out=a * np.exp(gamma*logr)
    return _normalize(out)
```
If g zeros, ratio huge -> clip. Good.

`_project_simplex`: Known algorithm. Ensure returns finite.
```
v=np.asarray(v,float); v=np.where(finite, v,0)
n=v.size
u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
cssv=np.cumsum(u)-1.0
cond = u*(np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)) -1.0 > cssv
if not np.any(cond): return np.ones(n)/n
idx=np.where(cond)[0][-1]
theta=cssv[idx]/(idx+1)
out=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
s=out.sum()
if s<=0: out=np.ones(n)/n else out/=s
return out
```
Good.

```
def _coordinate_polish(a,L,n,m,rng,time_limit,temp_v=0.08):
    deadline...
    a=normalize; c=conv; score=...
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    temp=temp_v; no_accept=0; recompute=0; accepts=0
    high_idx=np.arange(n); low_idx=high_idx
    factors=(0.05,0.12,0.25,0.45,0.7)
    while time<deadline:
       if recompute >= 20 or no_accept >= 100 or accepts >= 12:
           c=conv; score=...
           if score < best_score - 1e-12: update accepts=0
           v=n*c; w,_=weights(v,temp); g=pressure(w,a,L)
           q25,q75=np.percentile(g,[25,75]) if n>4 else (0,0)
           low_idx=np.where(g <= q25)[0]
           high_idx=np.where(g >= q75)[0]
           if low_idx.size < 20: low_idx=np.argsort(g)[:max(20,n//100)]
           if high_idx.size < 20: high_idx=np.argsort(g)[::-1][:max(20,n//100)]
           recompute=0; accepts=0
           if no_accept > 60:
               temp=max(0.02, temp*0.85)
           no_accept=0
       if high_idx.size==0 or low_idx.size==0:
           break
       i=int(high_idx[int(rng.integers(high_idx.size))])
       j=int(low_idx[int(rng.integers(low_idx.size))])
       if i==j: continue
       bi=float(a[i]); bj=float(a[j])
       if bi <=0: continue
       accepted=False
       for fac in factors:
           delta=fac*bi
           if delta <=0 or delta >= bi: continue
           a[i]=bi-delta; a[j]=bj+delta
           s2=_score_self(a,L,n,m)
           if s2 < score - 1e-12:
              score=s2; accepted=True; break
           a[i]=bi; a[j]=bj
       if accepted:
           if score < best_score - 1e-12: update best
           accepts += 1; no_accept=0
       else:
           no_accept += 1; recompute += 1
           if no_accept > 500:
              rho=0.03+0.08*float(rng.random())
              a=_normalize((1-rho)*a+rho*_random_prior(n,rng))
              no_accept=0; recompute=0; accepts=0
    c=conv(best_a); score=...
    return best_a, score
```
`np.argsort(g)[::-1]` returns descending indices. Good.
`np.percentile` on g with possible nonfinite? g finite. If not, replace.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2).

This implementation uses FFT-based exact scores, active-set softmax gradient
directions, mirror/Euclidean line searches, LP-based accentuated directions,
L-BFGS polishing, coordinate exchange polishing, and short local restarts.
The objective is scale invariant, so internally the sequence is normalized to
sum to 1 and the score is 2*n*max(conv(a, a)).
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _scipy_optimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_optimize = None

_LBFGS_DEADLINE = [float("inf")]


def _get_base():
    try:
        return height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        return None


def _normalize(a, force_nonnegative=True):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    if force_nonnegative:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a.fill(1.0)
        s = float(a.size)
    a /= s
    return a


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _conv_self(a, L):
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, int(L))
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, int(L))
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score_self(a, L, n, m):
    c = _conv_self(a, L)
    return float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))


def _weights_from_v(v, temp_v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    temp_v = float(max(1e-12, temp_v))
    vmax = float(np.max(v))
    z = (v - vmax) / temp_v
    np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0, out=z)
    w = np.exp(z)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
        w.fill(1.0 / float(v.size))
    else:
        w /= ws
    return w, vmax


def _pressure(u, a, L):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    U = np.fft.rfft(u, int(L))
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], int(L))
    y = np.fft.irfft(U * Ar, int(L))
    g = 2.0 * y[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    g = np.ascontiguousarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
    return g


def _mirror_update(a, d, step):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    step = float(step)
    if step == 0.0:
        return _normalize(a)
    log_factor = -step * d
    np.clip(log_factor, -70.0, 70.0, out=log_factor)
    out = a * np.exp(log_factor)
    return _normalize(out)


def _inverse_pressure_update(a, g, gamma):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 1e-300)
    gm = float(np.mean(g))
    if gm <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(gm):
        return _normalize(a)
    log_ratio = np.log(gm / g)
    np.clip(log_ratio, -50.0, 50.0, out=log_ratio)
    out = a * np.exp(float(gamma) * log_ratio)
    return _normalize(out)


def _project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    n = int(v.size)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u) - 1.0
    idx = np.where(u * (np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) - 1.0) > cssv)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        out = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
        s = float(out.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            out.fill(1.0 / float(n))
        else:
            out /= s
        return out
    j = int(idx[-1])
    theta = float(cssv[j] / float(j + 1))
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        out.fill(1.0 / float(n))
    else:
        out /= s
    return out


def _euc_update(a, d, step):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    v = a - float(step) * d
    return _project_simplex(v)


def _random_prior(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    ncomp = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
    for _ in range(ncomp):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.15)) / math.sqrt(float(max(1, freq)))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * t + phase)

    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, n)
    win = min(int(rng.integers(3, 9)), max(3, n // 12))
    if win > 1:
        kernel = np.ones(win, dtype=np.float64) / float(win)
        noise = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode="same")
    z += noise

    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(z - float(np.max(z)))
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    return _normalize(p)


def _shape_candidates(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    out = []
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    shapes = []

    shapes.append(np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))
    shapes.append(x.copy())
    shapes.append(-x.copy())
    shapes.append((x * x).copy())
    shapes.append(-(x * x).copy())

    for c in (0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.65, 0.80):
        shapes.append(1.6 * (t - c) * (t - c))
        shapes.append(-1.6 * (t - c) * (t - c))

    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8):
        for amp in (0.55, 0.95):
            for ph in (0.0, 0.25 * np.pi, 0.5 * np.pi, 0.75 * np.pi, np.pi, 1.5 * np.pi):
                shapes.append(amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + ph))

    for z in shapes:
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
        sc = float(np.max(np.abs(z)))
        if sc > 1e-12:
            z = z / sc * 0.90
        p = np.exp(z - float(np.max(z)))
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
        out.append(_normalize(p))

    for _ in range(10):
        out.append(_random_prior(n, rng))

    return out


def _add_candidate(arrays, scores, arr, score, max_len=24):
    if arr is None:
        return
    try:
        score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if not math.isfinite(score):
        return
    arr = _normalize(arr)
    if len(scores) < max_len:
        arrays.append(arr.copy())
        scores.append(score)
    else:
        worst = max(scores)
        if score < worst:
            idx = int(scores.index(worst))
            arrays[idx] = arr.copy()
            scores[idx] = score


def _active_search(a, L, n, m, rng, time_limit, temp0=0.25, temp_min=0.03):
    if time_limit is None:
        time_limit = 0.0
    time_limit = float(time_limit)
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        a = _normalize(a)
        return a, _score_self(a, int(L), int(n), int(m))

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, time_limit)
    a = _normalize(a)
    c = _conv_self(a, int(L))
    score = float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    temp_v = float(temp0)
    eta = 0.65
    no_improve = 0
    reset_count = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _conv_self(a, int(L))
        score = float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()

        v = float(n) * c
        w, _ = _weights_from_v(v, temp_v)

        ss = float(np.dot(w, w))
        neff = (1.0 / ss) if ss > 0.0 else 1.0
        if neff < 8.0:
            temp_v = min(1.25, temp_v * 1.25)
        elif neff > 600.0:
            temp_v = max(float(temp_min), temp_v * 0.90)
        if it % 180 == 0:
            temp_v = max(float(temp_min), temp_v * 0.975)

        g = _pressure(w, a, int(L))
        d = g - float(np.mean(g))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if scale <= 1e-300:
            no_improve += 1
            eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.80)
        else:
            d = d / scale
            cand_a = None
            cand_score = score

            for step in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta):
                cand = _mirror_update(a, d, step)
                s = _score_self(cand, int(L), int(n), int(m))
                if s < cand_score - 1e-12:
                    cand_score = s
                    cand_a = cand

            for gamma in (0.35, 0.85):
                cand = _inverse_pressure_update(a, g, gamma)
                s = _score_self(cand, int(L), int(n), int(m))
                if s < cand_score - 1e-12:
                    cand_score = s
                    cand_a = cand

            for step in (0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta):
                cand = _euc_update(a, d, step)
                s = _score_self(cand, int(L), int(n), int(m))
                if s < cand_score - 1e-12:
                    cand_score = s
                    cand_a = cand

            if cand_a is not None and cand_score < score - 1e-12:
                a = cand_a
                score = cand_score
                no_improve = 0
                eta = min(3.0, eta * 1.08)
            else:
                eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.78)
                no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 45:
            if reset_count < 8:
                a = best_a.copy()
                c = _conv_self(a, int(L))
                score = float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))
                eta = 0.65
                temp_v = max(float(temp_min), temp_v * 0.80)
                reset_count += 1
            else:
                rho = 0.05 + 0.20 * float(rng.random())
                a = _normalize((1.0 - rho) * a + rho * _random_prior(int(n), rng))
                c = _conv_self(a, int(L))
                score = float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))
                eta = 0.65
                temp_v = float(temp0)
                reset_count = 0
            no_improve = 0

        it += 1

    c = _conv_self(best_a, int(L))
    best_score = float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))
    return best_a, best_score


def _mix_line_search(a, d, L, n, m):
    a = _normalize(a)
    d = _normalize(d)
    best = a
    best_s = _score_self(a, int(L), int(n), int(m))
    for lam in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00):
        cand = _normalize((1.0 - lam) * a + lam * d)
        s = _score_self(cand, int(L), int(n), int(m))
        if s < best_s - 1e-12:
            best_s = s
            best = cand
    return best, best_s


def _lp_direction(a, c, n, k_active=100):
    if _scipy_optimize is None:
        return None
    try:
        n = int(n)
        k_active = int(min(max(10, k_active), 2 * n - 1))
        a = _normalize(a)
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(M):
            return None

        idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:k_active]
        A = np.zeros((int(idx.size), n), dtype=np.float64)

        for r, k in enumerate(idx):
            k = int(k)
            start = max(0, k - (n - 1))
            end = min(n - 1, k)
            ii = np.arange(start, end + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            A[r, ii] = a[k - ii]

        U = max(12.0 * M, 1e-15)
        cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b = np.full(int(idx.size), M, dtype=np.float64)

        res = _scipy_optimize.linprog(
            cvec,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=(0.0, U),
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )

        if res is not None and int(res.status) == 0 and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
            g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
            s = float(g.sum())
            if s > 0.0:
                return _normalize(g)
    except Exception:
        return None
    return None


def _coordinate_polish(a, L, n, m, rng, time_limit, temp_v=0.08):
    if time_limit is None:
        time_limit = 0.0
    time_limit = float(time_limit)
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        a = _normalize(a)
        return a, _score_self(a, int(L), int(n), int(m))

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, time_limit)
    a = _normalize(a)
    c = _conv_self(a, int(L))
    score = float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    temp = float(temp_v)
    no_accept = 0
    recompute = 0
    accepts = 0

    high_idx = np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.int64)
    low_idx = high_idx.copy()
    factors = (0.05, 0.12, 0.25, 0.45, 0.70)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if recompute >= 18 or no_accept >= 90 or accepts >= 12:
            c = _conv_self(a, int(L))
            score = float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))
            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
                accepts = 0

            v = float(n) * c
            w, _ = _weights_from_v(v, temp)
            g = _pressure(w, a, int(L))
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

            if int(n) > 8:
                q25 = float(np.percentile(g, 25))
                q75 = float(np.percentile(g, 75))
            else:
                q25 = 0.5 * float(np.mean(g))
                q75 = 1.5 * float(np.mean(g))

            low_idx = np.where(g <= q25)[0]
            high_idx = np.where(g >= q75)[0]

            if low_idx.size < 30:
                low_idx = np.argsort(g)[:max(30, int(n) // 100)]
            if high_idx.size < 30:
                high_idx = np.argsort(g)[::-1][:max(30, int(n) // 100)]

            recompute = 0
            accepts = 0
            if no_accept > 60:
                temp = max(0.018, temp * 0.85)
            no_accept = 0

        if high_idx.size == 0 or low_idx.size == 0:
            break

        i = int(high_idx[int(rng.integers(high_idx.size))])
        j = int(low_idx[int(rng.integers(low_idx.size))])
        if i == j:
            continue

        bi = float(a[i])
        bj = float(a[j])
        if bi <= 0.0:
            continue

        accepted = False
        for fac in factors:
            delta = float(fac) * bi
            if delta <= 0.0 or delta >= bi:
                continue
            a[i] = bi - delta
            a[j] = bj + delta
            s2 = _score_self(a, int(L), int(n), int(m))
            if s2 < score - 1e-12:
                score = s2
                accepted = True
                break
            a[i] = bi
            a[j] = bj

        if accepted:
            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
            accepts += 1
            no_accept = 0
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            recompute += 1
            if no_accept > 800:
                rho = 0.02 + 0.07 * float(rng.random())
                a = _normalize((1.0 - rho) * a + rho * _random_prior(int(n), rng))
                c = _conv_self(a, int(L))
                score = float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))
                no_accept = 0
                recompute = 0
                accepts = 0

    c = _conv_self(best_a, int(L))
    best_score = float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))
    return best_a, best_score


def _restart_search(a, L, n, m, rng, time_limit):
    if time_limit is None:
        time_limit = 0.0
    time_limit = float(time_limit)
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        a = _normalize(a)
        return a, _score_self(a, int(L), int(n), int(m))

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(2.0, time_limit)
    a = _normalize(a)
    c = _conv_self(a, int(L))
    best_score = float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))
    best_a = a.copy()
    attempts = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 8.0:
            break

        attempts += 1
        rho = 0.02 + 0.22 * float(rng.random())
        prior = _random_prior(int(n), rng)
        probe = _normalize((1.0 - rho) * a + rho * prior)

        lim = min(55.0, max(2.0, 0.25 * rem))
        a2, s2 = _active_search(probe, int(L), int(n), int(m), rng, lim, 0.16, 0.025)
        if s2 < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = s2
            best_a = a2.copy()

    c = _conv_self(best_a, int(L))
    best_score = float(2.0 * int(n) * float(np.max(c)))
    return best_a, best_score


def _softmax_center(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    theta = theta - float(np.mean(theta))
    theta = theta - float(np.max(theta))
    e = np.exp(theta)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.ones_like(theta)
        s = float(e.size)
    e /= s
    return e


def _lbfgs_fun(theta, L, n, m, temp_v):
    a = _softmax_center(theta)
    c = _conv_self(a, int(L))
    v = float(int(n)) * c
    vmax = float(np.max(v))
    z = (v - vmax) / float(temp_v)
    np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0, out=z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        f = vmax
        grad = np.zeros_like(theta, dtype=np.float64)
        return f, grad

    w = e / s
    f = float(vmax + float(temp_v) * math.log(s))

    g = _pressure(w, a, int(L))
    dot = float(np.dot(a, g))
    base = a * (g - dot)
    grad = float(int(n)) * base
    grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return f, grad


def _lbfgs_callback(*args):
    return time.monotonic() > float(_LBFGS_DEADLINE[0])


def _lbfgs_polish(a, L, n, m, temp_v, time_limit):
    if _scipy_optimize is None:
        return None
    if time_limit is None or float(time_limit) <= 2.0:
        return _normalize(a)

    try:
        a = _normalize(a)
        theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
        theta = theta - float(np.mean(theta))
        theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)

        stop_at = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(time_limit) - 2.0)
        _LBFGS_DEADLINE[0] = stop_at

        res = _scipy_optimize.minimize(
            _lbfgs_fun,
            theta,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            args=(int(L), int(n), int(m), float(temp_v)),
            jac=True,
            callback=_lbfgs_callback,
            options={
                "maxiter": 10000,
                "maxls": 20,
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-10,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            a2 = _softmax_center(res.x)
            if a2 is not None and a2.size == int(n) and np.all(np.isfinite(a2)):
                return _normalize(a2)
    except Exception:
        return None
    return None


def _internal_fft_score(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    c = _conv_self(a, L)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)))


def _exact_score(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except NameError:
        return _internal_fft_score(np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if val is None:
        return float("inf")
    return float(val)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not math.isfinite(budget) or budget < 10.0:
        budget = max(10.0, budget if math.isfinite(budget) else 2400.0)

    start = time.monotonic()
    hard = start + max(5.0, budget - 4.0)

    n = 6121

    # Fast path for tiny budgets.
    if budget < 30.0:
        base = _get_base()
        if base is not None:
            arr = _normalize(base)
            if arr.size == n:
                return [float(x) for x in arr]
            old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
            new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
            arr = _normalize(np.interp(new, old, arr))
            return [float(x) for x in arr]
        return [float(1.0 / n) for _ in range(n)]

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    m = 2 * n - 1

    cand_arrays = []
    cand_scores = []

    base = _get_base()
    if base is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s > 0.0:
                arr /= s
                if arr.size != n:
                    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
                    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
                    arr = np.interp(new, old, arr)
                    arr = _normalize(arr)
                else:
                    arr = _normalize(arr)
                sc = _score_self(arr, L, n, m)
                _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, arr, sc, 32)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not cand_arrays:
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        arr /= float(n)
        sc = _score_self(arr, L, n, m)
        _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, arr, sc, 32)

    for arr in _shape_candidates(n, rng):
        sc = _score_self(arr, L, n, m)
        _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, arr, sc, 32)

    order = sorted(range(len(cand_scores)), key=cand_scores.__getitem__)
    best_idx = int(order[0])
    best_a = cand_arrays[best_idx].copy()
    best_score = float(cand_scores[best_idx])
    best_a = _normalize(best_a)
    c = _conv_self(best_a, L)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)))
    _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, best_a, best_score, 32)

    print(f"n={n} L={L} initial best={best_score:.9f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    def _remaining():
        return max(0.0, hard - time.monotonic())

    # Main active-set search.
    if _remaining() > 80.0:
        lim = min(950.0, 0.42 * budget, _remaining() - 10.0)
        lim = max(15.0, lim)
        best_a, best_score = _active_search(best_a, L, n, m, rng, lim, 0.25, 0.030)
        _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, best_a, best_score, 32)
        print(f"after active-1 best={best_score:.9f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # L-BFGS polish, medium and hard.
    if _scipy_optimize is not None:
        for temp_v, max_sec in ((0.18, 220.0), (0.08, 180.0), (0.030, 150.0)):
            rem = _remaining()
            if rem < 40.0:
                break
            lim = min(max_sec, 0.14 * budget, rem - 10.0)
            if lim <= 5.0:
                break
            a2 = _lbfgs_polish(best_a, L, n, m, temp_v, lim)
            if a2 is not None:
                s2 = _score_self(a2, L, n, m)
                _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, a2, s2, 32)
                if s2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_a = a2
                    best_score = s2
                print(f"lbfgs temp={temp_v:.3f} best={best_score:.9f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # Second active-set pass with a harder tail.
    if _remaining() > 120.0:
        lim = min(650.0, 0.25 * budget, _remaining() - 10.0)
        lim = max(20.0, lim)
        best_a, best_score = _active_search(best_a, L, n, m, rng, lim, 0.12, 0.020)
        _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, best_a, best_score, 32)
        print(f"after active-2 best={best_score:.9f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # LP-based accentuated direction, if credible time remains.
    if _remaining() > 90.0:
        for K in (70, 160):
            rem = _remaining()
            if rem < 45.0:
                break
            try:
                c = _conv_self(best_a, L)
                d = _lp_direction(best_a, c, n, K)
                if d is None:
                    continue
                a2, s2 = _mix_line_search(best_a, d, L, n, m)
                _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, a2, s2, 32)
                if s2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_a = a2
                    best_score = s2
                    # Short polish after a good LP move.
                    rem2 = _remaining()
                    if rem2 > 30.0:
                        lim = min(45.0, 0.12 * rem2)
                        best_a, best_score = _active_search(
                            best_a, L, n, m, rng, lim, 0.10, 0.020
                        )
                        _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, best_a, best_score, 32)
                print(f"lp K={K} best={best_score:.9f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s", flush=True)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Coordinate exchange polishing.
    if _remaining() > 120.0:
        lim = min(350.0, 0.18 * budget, _remaining() - 10.0)
        lim = max(20.0, lim)
        best_a, best_score = _coordinate_polish(best_a, L, n, m, rng, lim, 0.065)
        _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, best_a, best_score, 32)
        print(f"after coordinate best={best_score:.9f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # Local restarts.
    if _remaining() > 110.0:
        lim = min(450.0, 0.22 * budget, _remaining() - 10.0)
        lim = max(20.0, lim)
        best_a, best_score = _restart_search(best_a, L, n, m, rng, lim)
        _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, best_a, best_score, 32)
        print(f"after restarts best={best_score:.9f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # Final brief active polish if time remains.
    if _remaining() > 90.0:
        lim = min(220.0, _remaining() - 20.0)
        lim = max(10.0, lim)
        best_a, best_score = _active_search(best_a, L, n, m, rng, lim, 0.08, 0.018)
        _add_candidate(cand_arrays, cand_scores, best_a, best_score, 32)

    # Exact evaluation of a small top set.
    evaluated_arrays = []
    seen_ids = set()
    if best_a is not None:
        best_a = _normalize(best_a)
        evaluated_arrays.append(best_a)
        seen_ids.add(id(best_a))

    order = sorted(range(len(cand_scores)), key=cand_scores.__getitem__)
    max_exact = 10 if n <= 7000 else 6
    for idx in order:
        if len(evaluated_arrays) >= max_exact:
            break
        arr = cand_arrays[idx]
        if id(arr) in seen_ids:
            continue
        seen_ids.add(id(arr))
        arr = _normalize(arr)
        evaluated_arrays.append(arr)

    best_seq = [float(x) for x in best_a]
    best_exact = float("inf")

    for arr in evaluated_arrays:
        if time.monotonic() > hard - 4.0:
            break
        seq = [float(x) for x in arr]
        val = _exact_score(seq)
        if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact:
            best_exact = val
            best_seq = seq

    if len(best_seq) != n:
        best_seq = [float(x) for x in _normalize(best_a)]

    final_c = _conv_self(_normalize(best_seq), L)
    final_internal = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(final_c)))
    print(f"final internal best={final_internal:.9f} exact_best={best_exact if math.isfinite(best_exact) else float('nan'):.9f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s", flush=True)

    return best_seq
```
